2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eThe decision log is always on. Each completed run appends its decision to \\u003ccode\\u003e~/.tradingagents/memory/trading_memory.md\\u003c/code\\u003e. On the next run for the same ticker, TradingAgents fetches the realised return (raw and alpha vs SPY), generates a one-paragraph reflection, and injects the most recent same-ticker decisions plus recent cross-ticker lessons into the Portfolio Manager prompt, so each analysis carries forward what worked and what didn't.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eOverride the path with \\u003ccode\\u003eTRADINGAGENTS_MEMORY_LOG_PATH\\u003c/code\\u003e.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch3 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eCheckpoint resume\\u003c/h3\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-checkpoint-resume\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: Checkpoint resume\\\" href=\\\"#checkpoint-resume\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eCheckpoint resume is opt-in via \\u003ccode\\u003e--checkpoint\\u003c/code\\u003e. When enabled, LangGraph saves state after each node so a crashed or interrupted run resumes from the last successful step instead of starting over. On a resume run you will see \\u003ccode\\u003eResuming from step N for \\u0026lt;TICKER\\u0026gt; on \\u0026lt;date\\u0026gt;\\u003c/code\\u003e in the logs; on a new run you will see \\u003ccode\\u003eStarting fresh\\u003c/code\\u003e. Checkpoints are cleared automatically on successful completion.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003ePer-ticker SQLite databases live at \\u003ccode\\u003e~/.tradingagents/cache/checkpoints/\\u0026lt;TICKER\\u0026gt;.db\\u003c/code\\u003e (override the base with \\u003ccode\\u003eTRADINGAGENTS_CACHE_DIR\\u003c/code\\u003e). Use \\u003ccode\\u003e--clear-checkpoints\\u003c/code\\u003e to reset all of them before a run.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\\\"tradingagents analyze --checkpoint           # enable for this run\\ntradingagents analyze --clear-checkpoints    # reset before running\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpre\\u003etradingagents analyze --checkpoint           \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c\\\"\\u003e\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c\\\"\\u003e#\\u003c/span\\u003e enable for this run\\u003c/span\\u003e\\ntradingagents analyze --clear-checkpoints    \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c\\\"\\u003e\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c\\\"\\u003e#\\u003c/span\\u003e reset before running\\u003c/span\\u003e\\u003c/pre\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\\\"config = DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()\\nconfig[\\u0026quot;checkpoint_enabled\\u0026quot;] = True\\nta = TradingAgentsGraph(config=config)\\n_, decision = class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eReproducibility\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eTradingAgents is LLM-driven, so two runs of the same ticker and date can differ. This is expected for a research tool built on language models, not a defect. The variation comes from a few distinct sources, and it helps to separate them.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eLanguage model sampling is non-deterministic. Even at a fixed temperature, providers do not guarantee byte-identical output across calls, and reasoning models (the default GPT-5.x family, and any thinking-mode model) vary the most because their internal reasoning is itself sampled.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eLive data moves. News, StockTwits, and Reddit return different content as time passes, so a run today sees different inputs than a run last week even for the same historical trade date. Pin the analysis date to hold the price and indicator window fixed, but the social and news sources still reflect \\\"now\\\".\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eTo reduce variation you can lower the sampling temperature. Set \\u003ccode\\u003etemperature\\u003c/code\\u003e in your config (or \\u003ccode\\u003eTRADINGAGENTS_TEMPERATURE\\u003c/code\\u003e in \\u003ccode\\u003e.env\\u003c/code\\u003e); lower values make models that honor it more repeatable. Reasoning models largely ignore temperature, so for tighter reproducibility pair a low temperature with a non-reasoning model such as \\u003ccode\\u003egpt-4.1\\u003c/code\\u003e.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\\\"config = DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()\\nconfig[\\u0026quot;llm_provider\\u0026quot;] = \\u0026quot;openai\\u0026quot;\\nconfig[\\u0026quot;deep_think_llm\\u0026quot;] = \\u0026quot;gpt-4.1\\u0026quot;      # non-reasoning model honors temperature\\nconfig[\\u0026quot;quick_think_llm\\u0026quot;] = \\u0026quot;gpt-4.1\\u0026quot;\\nconfig[\\u0026quot;temperature\\u0026quot;] = 0.0\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpre\\u003e\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s1\\\"\\u003econfig\\u003c/span\\u003e \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003e=\\u003c/span\\u003e \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003eDEFAULT_CONFIG\\u003c/span\\u003e.\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003ecopy\\u003c/span\\u003e()\\n\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s1\\\"\\u003econfig\\u003c/span\\u003e[\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s\\\"\\u003e\\\"llm_provider\\\"\\u003c/span\\u003e] \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003e=\\u003c/span\\u003e \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s\\\"\\u003e\\\"openai\\\"\\u003c/span\\u003e\\n\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s1\\\"\\u003econfig\\u003c/span\\u003e[\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s\\\"\\u003e\\\"deep_think_llm\\\"\\u003c/span\\u003e] \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003e=\\u003c/span\\u003e \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s\\\"\\u003e\\\"gpt-4.1\\\"\\u003c/span\\u003e      \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c\\\"\\u003e# non-reasoning model honors temperature\\u003c/span\\u003e\\n\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s1\\\"\\u003econfig\\u003c/span\\u003e[\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s\\\"\\u003e\\\"quick_think_llm\\\"\\u003c/span\\u003e] \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003e=\\u003c/span\\u003e \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s\\\"\\u003e\\\"gpt-4.1\\\"\\u003c/span\\u003e\\n\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s1\\\"\\u003econfig\\u003c/span\\u003e[\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-s\\\"\\u003e\\\"temperature\\\"\\u003c/span\\u003e] \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003e=\\u003c/span\\u003e \\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003e0.0\\u003c/span\\u003e\\u003c/pre\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eWhat does not vary anymore: the analyzed company identity is resolved deterministically from the ticker before any agent runs, and the market analyst grounds exact price and indicator claims in a verified data snapshot. Earlier reports of \\\"different companies\\\" or fabricated price levels across runs are addressed by these two mechanisms.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eBacktest results are not guaranteed to match any published figure. Returns depend on the model, the temperature, the date range, data quality, and the sampling above. Treat the framework as a research scaffold for studying multi-agent analysis, not as a strategy with a fixed, replicable return.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" Looking forward to building impactful projects with you!</p>\n</blockquote>\n<div align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\">\n<p dir=\"auto\">🚀 <a href=\"#tradingagents-framework\">TradingAgents</a> | ⚡ <a href=\"#installation-and-cli\">Installation &amp; CLI</a> | 🎬 <a href=\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=90gr5lwjIho\" rel=\"nofollow\">Demo</a> | 📦 <a href=\"#tradingagents-package\">Package Usage</a> | 🤝 <a href=\"#contributing\">Contributing</a> | 📄 <a href=\"#citation\">Citation</a></p>\n</div>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">TradingAgents Framework</h2><a id=\"user-content-tradingagents-framework\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: TradingAgents Framework\" href=\"#tradingagents-framework\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">TradingAgents is a multi-agent trading framework that mirrors the dynamics of real-world trading firms. By deploying specialized LLM-powered agents: from fundamental analysts, sentiment experts, and technical analysts, to trader, risk management team, the platform collaboratively evaluates market conditions and informs trading decisions. Moreover, these agents engage in dynamic discussions to pinpoint the optimal strategy.</p>\n<p align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\">\n  <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/TauricResearch/TradingAgents/blob/main/assets/schema.png\"><img src=\"/TauricResearch/TradingAgents/raw/main/assets/schema.png\" style=\"width: 100%; max-width: 100%;\"></a>\n</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">TradingAgents framework is designed for research purposes. Trading performance may vary based on many factors, including the chosen backbone language models, model temperature, trading periods, the quality of data, and other non-deterministic factors. <a href=\"https://tauric.ai/disclaimer/\" rel=\"nofollow\">It is not intended as financial, investment, or trading advice.</a></p>\n</blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Our framework decomposes complex trading tasks into specialized roles.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Analyst Team</h3><a id=\"user-content-analyst-team\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Analyst Team\" href=\"#analyst-team\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Fundamentals Analyst: Evaluates company financials and performance metrics, identifying intrinsic values and potential red flags.</li>\n<li>Sentiment Analyst: Aggregates news headlines, StockTwits, and Reddit chatter into a single sentiment read to gauge short-term market mood.</li>\n<li>News Analyst: Monitors global news and macroeconomic indicators, interpreting the impact of events on market conditions.</li>\n<li>Technical Analyst: Utilizes technical indicators (like MACD and RSI) to detect trading patterns and forecast price movements.</li>\n</ul>\n<p align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\">\n  <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/TauricResearch/TradingAgents/blob/main/assets/analyst.png\"><img critically assess the insights provided by the Analyst Team. Through structured debates, they balance potential gains against inherent risks.</li>\n</ul>\n<p align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\">\n  <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Composes reports from the analysts and researchers to make informed trading decisions, determining the timing and magnitude of trades.</li>\n</ul>\n<p align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\">\n  <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/TauricResearch/TradingAgents/blob/main/assets/trader.png\"><img and Portfolio Manager\" href=\"#risk-management-and-portfolio-manager\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Continuously evaluates portfolio risk by assessing market volatility, liquidity, and other risk factors. The risk management team evaluates and adjusts trading strategies, providing assessment reports to the Portfolio Manager for final decision.</li>\n<li>The Portfolio Manager approves/rejects the transaction proposal. If approved, the order will be sent to the simulated exchange and executed.</li>\n</ul>\n<p align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\">\n  <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" (e.g. Azure OpenAI, AWS Bedrock), copy <code>.env.enterprise.example</code> to <code>.env.enterprise</code> and fill in your credentials.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">For local models, configure Ollama with <code>llm_provider: \"ollama\"</code>. The default endpoint is <code>http://localhost:11434/v1</code>; set <code>OLLAMA_BASE_URL</code> to point at a remote <code>ollama-serve</code>. Pull models with <code>ollama pull &lt;name&gt;</code>, and pick \"Custom model ID\" in the CLI for any model not listed by default.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Alternatively, copy <code>.env.example</code> to <code>.env</code> and fill in your keys:</p>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\"><pre>cp .env.example .env</pre><div exchange-suffixed ticker. Company identity and the alpha benchmark resolve automatically per market.</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>US: <code>AAPL</code>, <code>SPY</code></li>\n<li>Hong Kong: <code>0700.HK</code> · Tokyo: <code>7203.T</code> · London: <code>AZN.L</code></li>\n<li>India: <code>RELIANCE.NS</code>, <code>.BO</code> · Canada: <code>.TO</code> · Australia: <code>.AX</code></li>\n<li>China A-shares: Shanghai <code>.SS</code>, Shenzhen <code>.SZ</code> (e.g. <code>600519.SS</code> for Kweichow Moutai)</li>\n<li>Crypto: <code>BTC-USD</code>, <code>ETH-USD</code></li>\n</ul>\n<p align=\"center\" LangGraph to ensure flexibility and modularity. The framework supports multiple LLM providers: OpenAI, Google, Anthropic, xAI, DeepSeek, Qwen (Alibaba DashScope, international and China endpoints), GLM (Zhipu), MiniMax (global + China), OpenRouter, Ollama for local models, and Azure OpenAI for enterprise.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Python Usage</h3><a id=\"user-content-python-usage\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Python Usage\" href=\"#python-usage\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">To use TradingAgents inside your code, you can import the <code>tradingagents</code> module and initialize a <code>TradingAgentsGraph()</code> object. The <code>.propagate()</code> function will return a decision. 1.06 0Z\"></path>\n</svg>\n    </clipboard-copy>\n  </div></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">See <code>tradingagents/default_config.py</code> for all configuration options.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Persistence and Recovery</h2><a id=\"user-content-persistence-and-recovery\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Persistence and Recovery\" href=\"#persistence-and-recovery\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" dir=\"auto\">TradingAgents persists two kinds of state across runs.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Decision log</h3><a id=\"user-content-decision-log\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Decision log\" href=\"#decision-log\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The decision log is always on. Each completed run appends its decision to <code>~/.tradingagents/memory/trading_memory.md</code>. On the next run for the same ticker, TradingAgents fetches the realised return (raw and alpha vs SPY), generates a one-paragraph reflection, and injects the most recent same-ticker decisions plus recent cross-ticker lessons into the Portfolio Manager prompt, so each analysis carries forward what worked and what didn't.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Override the path with <code>TRADINGAGENTS_MEMORY_LOG_PATH</code>.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Checkpoint resume</h3><a id=\"user-content-checkpoint-resume\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Checkpoint resume\" href=\"#checkpoint-resume\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Checkpoint resume is opt-in via <code>--checkpoint</code>. When enabled, LangGraph saves state after each node so a crashed or interrupted run resumes from the last successful step instead of starting over. On a resume run you will see <code>Resuming from step N for &lt;TICKER&gt; on &lt;date&gt;</code> in the logs; on a new run you will see <code>Starting fresh</code>. Checkpoints are cleared automatically on successful completion.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Per-ticker SQLite databases live at <code>~/.tradingagents/cache/checkpoints/&lt;TICKER&gt;.db</code> (override the base with <code>TRADINGAGENTS_CACHE_DIR</code>). Use <code>--clear-checkpoints</code> to reset all of them before a run.</p>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\"><pre>tradingagents class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Reproducibility</h2><a id=\"user-content-reproducibility\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Reproducibility\" href=\"#reproducibility\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">TradingAgents is LLM-driven, so two runs of the same ticker and date can differ. This is expected for a research tool built on language models, not a defect. The variation comes from a few distinct sources, and it helps to separate them.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Language model sampling is non-deterministic. Even at a fixed temperature, providers do not guarantee byte-identical output across calls, and reasoning models (the default GPT-5.x family, and any thinking-mode model) vary the most because their internal reasoning is itself sampled.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Live data moves. News, StockTwits, and Reddit return different content as time passes, so a run today sees different inputs than a run last week even for the same historical trade date. Pin the analysis date to hold the price and indicator window fixed, but the social and news sources still reflect \"now\".</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">To reduce variation you can lower the sampling temperature. Set <code>temperature</code> in your config (or <code>TRADINGAGENTS_TEMPERATURE</code> in <code>.env</code>); lower values make models that honor it more repeatable. Reasoning models largely ignore temperature, so for tighter reproducibility pair a low temperature with a non-reasoning model such as <code>gpt-4.1</code>.</p>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\"><pre><span class=\"pl-s1\">config</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">DEFAULT_CONFIG</span>.<span class=\"pl-c1\">copy</span>()\n<span class=\"pl-s1\">config</span>[<span class=\"pl-s\">\"llm_provider\"</span>] <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-s\">\"openai\"</span>\n<span class=\"pl-s1\">config</span>[<span class=\"pl-s\">\"deep_think_llm\"</span>] <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-s\">\"gpt-4.1\"</span>      <span class=\"pl-c\"># non-reasoning model honors temperature</span>\n<span class=\"pl-s1\">config</span>[<span class=\"pl-s\">\"quick_think_llm\"</span>] <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-s\">\"gpt-4.1\"</span>\n<span class=\"pl-s1\">config</span>[<span class=\"pl-s\">\"temperature\"</span>] <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">0.0</span></pre><div class=\"zeroclipboard-container\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to clipboard\" class=\"ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center\" data-copy-feedback=\"Copied!\" data-tooltip-direction=\"w\" value=\"config = DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()\nconfig[&quot;llm_provider&quot;] = &quot;openai&quot;\nconfig[&quot;deep_think_llm&quot;] = &quot;gpt-4.1&quot;      # non-reasoning model honors temperature\nconfig[&quot;quick_think_llm&quot;] = &quot;gpt-4.1&quot;\nconfig[&quot;temperature&quot;] = 0.0\" tabindex=\"0\" role=\"button\">\n      <svg aria-hidden=\"true\" data-component=\"Octicon\" height=\"16\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" data-view-component=\"true\" class=\"octicon octicon-copy js-clipboard-copy-icon\">\n    <path d=\"M0 6.75C0 5.784.784 5 1.75 5h1.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-1.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v7.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h7.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-1.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0v1.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 9.25 16h-7.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 14.25Z\"></path><path d=\"M5 1.75C5 .784 5.784 0 6.75 0h7.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v7.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 11h-7.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 5 9.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v7.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h7.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-7.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Z\"></path>\n</svg>\n      <svg aria-hidden=\"true\" data-component=\"Octicon\" height=\"16\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" data-view-component=\"true\" class=\"octicon octicon-check js-clipboard-check-icon color-fg-success d-none\">\n    <path d=\"M13.78 4.22a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.06l-7.25 7.25a.75.75 0 0 1-1.06 0L2.22 9.28a.751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018L6 10.94l6.72-6.72a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0Z\"></path>\n</svg>\n    </clipboard-copy>\n  </div></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">What does not vary anymore: the analyzed company identity is resolved deterministically from the ticker before any agent runs, and the market analyst grounds exact price and indicator claims in a verified data snapshot. Earlier reports of \"different companies\" or fabricated price levels across runs are addressed by these two mechanisms.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Backtest results are not guaranteed to match any published figure. Returns depend on the model, the temperature, the date range, data quality, and the sampling above. Treat the framework as a research scaffold for studying multi-agent analysis, not as a strategy with a fixed, replicable return.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\"
